## 2026-08-19 21:32 America/New_York — Restore the full spike/blurb entry objective

Decision:

- The narrow 41-window direct-source response replay is a diagnostic and does
  not replace the original movement-first news-blurb project.
- Rebuild the complete significant-movement surface across all 68 instruments
  and the retained 1-minute through 30-day horizons.
- Preserve both views: movement-first ex-post attribution and event-first
  causal entry replay.
- A fresh blurb or official event opens a currency watch. Direction may be
  supplied by a synchronized observed currency-factor change rather than being
  forced from prose. The entry timestamp is the first causal detection time,
  not the event time or the hindsight move start.
- Select the lowest-cost liquid pair expressing the detected strong-versus-weak
  currency factor, then measure remaining executable movement, MFE, MAE,
  continuation lifetime, reversal risk, and cost clearance from that entry.
- Learn separate immediate-continuation, pullback/resumption, delayed-break,
  spike/fade, conflict, and no-response mappings by event class and liquidity.
- Restore or explicitly classify the 7,048 legacy significant-move labels whose
  executable timestamps are presently unavailable; never silently discard them.

Safety/evidence disposition:

- Research and shadow only. No execution, authorization, promotion, or
  real-money routing changes.
- Ex-post or late blurbs remain explanatory labels only. Entry features must be
  point-in-time causal and outcomes must use executable bid/ask economics.
- Any selected entry rule requires factor/episode deduplication, cost stress,
  multiplicity control, and untouched prospective confirmation.

## 2026-08-19 21:38 America/New_York — Preserve pure-source factor discovery

Decision:

- Historical movement review must work backward from each material currency
  move to the purest available cause: the original official release,
  central-bank statement, policy action, intervention record, government fact,
  or directly measured market variable. A media article is a discovery pointer
  and timestamped narrative observation, not automatically the causal source.
- Extract the smallest measurable factor that changed: rate action, expected
  policy path, actual/prior/revision, inflation or labour delta, intervention
  amount, reserve or balance-sheet change, yield repricing, commodity shock,
  funding change, or a versioned statement/speech delta.
- Preserve numeric value, units, prior state, expected sign mechanism,
  knowledge time, provenance, confidence, and affected currencies. When no
  numeric factor exists, retain a versioned semantic factor with lower evidence
  grade and explicit uncertainty rather than a generic sentiment score.
- Map that factor to the realized currency-strength response across all pair
  legs at multiple horizons, separating common currency movement from
  pair-specific residuals. Repeated cases form factor-response analogs used to
  estimate onset, magnitude, continuation, reversal, and entryable remainder.
- Historical backward attribution is hypothesis discovery. Live use may read
  only the factor and market observations known at the decision timestamp.

## 2026-08-21 07:10 America/New_York — Practice 006 becomes a 21-currency rank challenger

- Removed the active Practice-006 identity as a riskier/looser copy of the
  news-plus-technical Practice-007 concept. The old news challenger code and
  historical evidence remain preserved, but the always-on supervisor no longer
  launches it and the dashboard no longer labels or reads it as the live 006
  strategy.
- Added a pure rank model over the exact **21-currency / 68-pair** executable
  universe. Each currency receives a robust standardized score from 5-minute
  (50%), 15-minute (35%), and 60-minute (15%) factor strength. A pair is
  eligible only when one leg is positive on both 5m and 15m, the other is
  negative on both, the real pair reaction agrees on both horizons, and its
  movement proxy clears 1.2x stressed current spread. These values describe
  the fixed Practice-006 experiment; they are not a validated production edge.
- Candidate allocation is factor-deduplicated: a greedy strongest-versus-
  weakest selection cannot reuse either currency, including currencies already
  reserved by any open account trade. Re-entry uses one attempt per continuous
  thesis and resets only after the qualifying rank thesis actually disappears;
  no arbitrary post-exit timer was added.
- Kept hard execution boundaries: OANDA practice endpoint, exact account suffix
  `-006`, fixed 100 units, exact model/lane/universe binding at the last order
  hook, maximum eight positions, maximum 48 daily fills, $1 session-loss stop,
  live quote/slippage gates, broker stop/trailing/time protection, and no
  real-money route. Practice 007 and its governed lifecycle are unchanged.
- Rewired the hidden supervisor, state/heartbeat, SQLite ledger, JSONL log, and
  dashboard. The live panel now shows strongest/weakest currency tables,
  disjoint selected pairs, 5-minute reaction, cost-cover ratio, positions,
  fills, exits, errors, and rejection reasons.
- The first live minutes exposed and repaired one operational logic defect:
  current entry-cost clearance had also been used as rank validity, causing a
  trade to close when the rolling five-minute move stopped clearing spread even
  though its currency ordering had not necessarily reversed. Entry and exit
  contracts are now separate. Cost/reaction checks govern entry; only a true
  strong-versus-weak score reversal (or reversal on both 5m and 15m), broker
  protection, or the one-hour horizon governs exit. Source loss cannot itself
  close a trade or reset its thesis generation.
- Validation: Python compilation and PowerShell parsing passed; **71 focused

Built 3,645,778 original minute origins across all 68 pairs, in 544 core/peer partition pairs, with 216 local plus 12 exact-clock peer inputs. Training/validation/later-development counts are 2,734,524 / 455,240 / 456,014. These dates were examined before; no untouched confirmation is claimed and the full approximately 53.5-million-row archive is not materialized.

Training-only input diagnostics used all training origins for constants/missingness and 91,288 original-clock samples for correlations. All 228 inputs vary somewhere; 42 strongly correlated relationships involve 52 fields. No correlation drops, feature-to-outcome selection or model fits were performed. Earlier ARIMA/Ridge/HGB/conditional comparisons are mapped in the new guide; the old ARIMA wrapper's origin alignment must be corrected for the future matched comparison.

A separate endpoint-only overlay preserves the base and all strict-path labels. It reread the same original source ranges and matched all 68 input receipts, with 60,962,440 finite strict numerical cells compared bit-for-bit. H1 measurable endpoint returns increased from 2,710,875 complete-path rows to 3,504,026 exact-endpoint rows; intervening missing quotes remain unknown. Every original row is preserved, and future validity remains a label mask, never an entry feature.

Validation: 92 focused tests plus 30 subtests passed. Base/overlay builders read back every written table and preserve numeric bits; independent all-partition acceptance receipts are retained with the guide. Recorded derived data is about 4.40 GiB combined, excluding small metadata and the existing live store. Existing collection, trading configuration, old model weights, raw archives and historical evidence remain unchanged. No profitability improvement or whole-project operational acceptance is claimed.

Automatic approval review blocked cleanup of a small synthetic-test temporary directory with a generic policy rejection. It was left in place; no alternate cleanup route was attempted. Source, exact manifests, input diagnostics, acceptance and recreation: docs/FOREX_TRAINING_WINDOWS_20260915.md and docs/validation/rolling_training_windows_20260915/.


## 2026-09-15 — completed rolling technical model comparison

Recorded 2026-09-15T18:35:46.304534+00:00. Continued the accepted rolling dataset into 32 fixed Ridge/boosting fits (38/50/216/228 inputs, 5/15/30/60m) and 20 controls. Fit on six TRAIN weeks, assess all 911,254 original minutes in two later development weeks; these dates were previously examined. Train-only per-pair normalization uses all 2,734,524 TRAIN origins before 15-minute clock sampling and mature-label selection. Current quote costs and exact delayed-entry targets are independently source-bound.

Caught a small pre-start-context inclusion in the auxiliary conditional-OLS ARIMA fit before learned fitting. Stopped and invalidated only that incomplete V1 panel; preserved original source/manifest, then rebuilt V2 with exact TRAIN-start scope. No historical source data or live collector was stopped or changed.

All 32 fits and 20 controls completed in 1,011.641 seconds. All 64 learned period assessments lose to no-change MAE; three later H1 boosting assessments slightly improve RMSE. Primary-cost nonoverlap means: 3 positive, 61 negative; no policy positive in both periods, including quoted-cost-only comparisons and separate path cohorts. Compact Ridge H1 later +1.984 bps over 46 decisions survives cost/delay but reverses after excluding its best day or pair and loses earlier. Compact-plus-peer boosting 30m later +0.891 bps quoted-only becomes -0.109 with 1 extra bp. All eight quoted-positive cases are retained, not discarded by a direction-accuracy cutoff.

Core 55 tests plus 19 subtests, auditor 7 tests and summary 10 tests passed. Independent audit verified 52 forecast files/47,385,208 original key rows, bounded saved replay of 32 models, exact recreation of 20 controls, 272 prior records, fit hashes, decision masks, mean costs and unchanged endpoint receipts. Saved estimators, normalizers, recipe/source bindings and full comparison tables are retained for recreation and avoiding repeat research. Scoped source/weight/evidence supplement is published with its own vault readback receipt.

No promotion, broker calls, live model/input changes or trading-setting changes. News/management remain deferred. Next work reuses existing specialist heads with training-only, time-ordered, horizon-purged out-of-fold outputs on this contract, checks feature-family contribution and separately timed evidence; it does not repeat this fixed mean-return sweep. Full details and bounded acceptance: docs/FOREX_ROLLING_MODEL_COMPARISON_20260915.md.


## 2026-09-15 — chronological rolling specialist comparison completed

Recorded 2026-09-15T20:57:49.386233+00:00. Reused the retained direction/conditional-magnitude/asymmetric-cost targets on compact38/50 rolling inputs at 30/60m, with four strictly earlier OOF folds and separate raw-only combining inputs. Completed 20 six-head bundles, 16 meta models, 4 calibrators and 28 variants over 911,254 original assessment origins. Both gates retain all 0/1/2-bp and delayed-entry outcomes, path cohorts, concentration and selected-return/cost error decomposition. At +1 bp each gate has 4 positive, 47 negative and 5 inactive full-endpoint period cases; none is positive in both development weeks even quoted-only. Retained limited positives: compact38 M30 direct learned reversal (+5.021bps later,25  decisions, no validation entries, strongest-day exclusion negative), M30 specialist Ridge (+0.823 validation, then−9.574), H1 calibrated mixture (+0.579 later, validation −4.664, cost/dominant-day sensitive), H1 direct-context (+0.401 later, delayed negative).

Posthoc same-row TRAIN reference comparisons show movement-size RMSE gains 6.60–8.26% in all 8 full-endpoint contexts/periods; median-based MAE improves only later. Exit-cost heads beat TRAIN references and current-wing persistence; these component gains do not establish a profitable entry model. The audited source retains 100 passing tests, 138 artifacts, 20 six-head and 16 meta replays, 4 calibrators and 16,339 scalar cost cases. No old results or live settings changed. Next is distinct historical rolling periods and family contribution; news/management remain deferred. See docs/FOREX_ROLLING_SPECIALISTS_20260915.md and its bound acceptance, summary and audit.

## September 15, 2026 — earlier historical replication and H1 family contribution completed

Completed windows March 23–May 18 and May 18–July 13: 1,827,574 original assessment origins, 27 six-head bundles per window, 20 full-context variants, 16 family means and contemporaneous original-recipe comparators/controls. The initial full grids finished, but the first family fit exposed scikit-learn 1.9.0 empty-column binning incompatibility. Preserved failed `_v1` evidence and all 156 existing artifacts per window; a pinned process-scoped repair continued only unfinished families into fresh `_v2` outputs. No recipe or selection change was made from partial performance. Both completed runs and independent recovery audits passed, including saved-model/forecast/cost/source checks; 172 research tests passed.

Repeated component value: expected-absolute RMSE improved against TRAIN means in all 16 new context/week cells, and exit-cost estimates beat historical and current-wing persistence references throughout. No full-endpoint specialist/family policy was positive in both weeks at quoted costs plus 1 bp. Retain the July 6–13 H1 removed-trend raw-mixture lead (+2.5126 bp at +1; +1.7970 with entry delay; n72), along with its three losing weeks. The original compact50 Ridge30 positive May week had only 36.99% wins, demonstrating why no 50% screen was imposed; it was concentrated and failed later. Family removal supports activity/spread history for movement and calendar for costs, with mixed signed-return effects.

See [final study guide](docs/FOREX_ROLLING_PERIOD_REPLICATION_20260915.md), complete separate-period summary, exact audits and execution receipts. These dates were examined historically: development replication, not untouched confirmation or the full archive. News and management remain deferred; no model promotion, order or live configuration change. The storage checkpoint is separately sealed. Exact source/models/normalizers and recreation receipts are prepared for the dated vault package; large raw/prepared/forecast dependencies remain local.
